### Files changed in this commit but out of Story 12.15's scope

- **`references/Hexalith.EventStore` working-tree changes (2026-08-25), disclosed because they are in another repository and are deliberately uncommitted.** `src/Hexalith.EventStore.ServiceDefaults/BoundedTelemetryShutdownService.cs` (new), `src/Hexalith.EventStore.ServiceDefaults/Extensions.cs` (registers it alongside `UseOtlpExporter()`), and `tests/Hexalith.EventStore.Server.Tests/Telemetry/BoundedTelemetryShutdownServiceTests.cs` (new, falsifiable regression coverage). Added in the same working tree by the later pass: `src/Hexalith.EventStore.Server/Projections/ProjectionCheckpointTracker.cs` (structural checkpoint-refusal reason codes and their log) and `tests/Hexalith.EventStore.Server.Tests/Projections/ProjectionCheckpointRefusalDiagnosticsTests.cs` (new, falsifiable). Authorised by Jerome's Decision 1 and made in the repository that owns the change. **Corrected 2026-08-26 (/bmad-code-review chunk D): this paragraph's original claim was false at `HEAD`.** It read "Nothing was committed or pushed there and the gitlink is unmoved at `49b84d0c`". Verified: `git ls-tree HEAD references/Hexalith.EventStore` gives `64a21632e38ef48b64041f9e12a076848cc4dbcb`, the submodule worktree is clean, and `git -C references/Hexalith.EventStore branch -r --contains 64a21632` returns `origin/main` — so the changes are committed **and pushed**, and the gitlink moved. The original "local-only commit would break the hosted runner" rationale is therefore moot: the commit is fetchable. This is the second consecutive occurrence of this defect (the previous pass closed it as `da52e2c8…` vs `6b9b349a…`), so the underlying problem is that a gitlink SHA is written into prose that is not re-derived when the pointer moves — treat any SHA recorded here as a point-in-time observation, not a standing claim. No nested submodule was initialised or updated. **Four further `references/*` gitlinks also moved in this delta and were previously undisclosed:** `Hexalith.Folders` (`a69dd84b`→`b583d3a7`), `Hexalith.FrontComposer` (`f3552343`→`23b0b556`), `Hexalith.Projects` (`89c53002`→`007d74bf`) and `Hexalith.Tenants` (`09c746b3`→`865a80ac`) — so the original "no other `references/*` entry was touched" was also false. None is Story 12.15 work; they are recorded here for disclosure.

- **Hosted-lane unblocking changes (2026-08-25), disclosed rather than claimed as Story 12.15 work.** `tests/PackageCatalogTestHelper.cs` — ratified four UI foundation pins the shared catalog had already moved (`Microsoft.FluentUI.AspNetCore.Components` `5.0.0-rc.4-26180.1`→`5.0.0-rc.5-26219.1`, `Fluxor` `6.10.0`→`6.11.0`, `Microsoft.Playwright` `1.61.0`→`1.62.0`, `bunit` `2.8.4-preview`→`2.9.0`); the helper throws on the first mismatch, so only the Fluent row was visible until it was fixed. `tests/fixtures/hexalith-chatbot-generated-client.sha256` — refreshed to the committed generated client; the stale value was the hash at `4366a6f`, left behind when `1d02b69` regenerated the client, making this a third undisclosed consequence of that same unrelated commit. `tests/Hexalith.ChatBot.UI.E2E.Tests/RealRenderCrossSurfaceE2ETests.cs` — the Story 13.9 skip-link assertion required an exact `href="#fc-main-content"`, but the FrontComposer shell now emits a route-qualified fragment (`MainContentHref => GetCurrentRouteFragmentHref("fc-main-content")`); the assertion matches the fragment suffix instead, preserving its intent. `_bmad-output/planning-artifacts/architecture.md`, `_bmad-output/planning-artifacts/epics.md` and `tests/Hexalith.ChatBot.Architecture.Tests/ScaffoldArchitectureTests.cs` — commit `f804229` ratified the shared catalog's `ModelContextProtocol` `1.4.1`→`2.2.0` **major** bump by editing the expected-version assertion and the two planning records, with no ADR entry, no decision-log record, and no change under `src/Hexalith.ChatBot.Mcp`. The pinned values do match the current `references/Hexalith.Builds` catalog, so this is a governance gap rather than drift. Disclosed here (2026-08-25 review) rather than claimed as Story 12.15 work; ratifying the major bump belongs to the package-authority story, and a durable entry is carried in `deferred-work.md`. These belong to Epic 13 / the package-authority story, not here. The 42 Story 13.9 screenshot fixtures that the UI E2E suite rewrites as a side effect were deliberately reverted rather than swept into this commit.
- `.github/workflows/ci.yml` (build step) — `-m:1` added to serialize project builds. This file is already in Story 12.15's File List for the recovery lanes, but this particular edit is general build hygiene, not recovery work: several referenced submodule projects are reachable by more than one path in the graph and parallel nodes intermittently raced writing their `obj/` outputs.

- `_bmad-output/implementation-artifacts/tests/screenshots/story-13.9/*.png` — 42 regenerated Playwright screenshots. Running the UI E2E suite rewrites these fixtures, so they were swept into commit `1838eba` alongside the recovery work. They belong to Story 13.9, are not part of this story's change set, and are listed here only so the commit's contents are fully disclosed rather than partially.
- `src/Hexalith.ChatBot.Server/Gateway/CommandGatewayServiceCollectionExtensions.cs:297` — one line, `services.TryAddSingleton<ICorrectionPropagationWorkflowStatusSink, OperationStatusWorkflowStatusSink>();`. Introduced by commit `1d02b69` ("build: sync local changes via /pushall"), which bundled it with unrelated changes (new correction-propagation-status-sink types, `Queries/OperationStatus.cs`, a regenerated NSwag client, an OpenAPI yaml update) that landed between this story's baseline and its later commits. Not part of Story 12.15's File List or work. Kept as-is (2026-08-24 review decision) rather than reverted, since `1d02b69` is already on `main` and reverting would reintroduce the silent no-op-sink fallback it fixed; listed here so the commit's contents are fully disclosed.
- **The rest of commit `1d02b69`'s footprint, including its test coverage** (2026-08-24, chunk D review). The full commit is a 38-file/768-line, fully self-contained, unrelated feature: a Dapr-workflow correction-propagation runner (`CorrectionPropagationWorkflowRunner.cs`, `ICorrectionPropagationWorkflowSteps.cs`), duplicate-instance detection (`DaprWorkflowDuplicateInstanceDetector.cs`), and the operation-status sink named above (`OperationStatusWorkflowStatusSink.cs`, `NullCorrectionPropagationWorkflowStatusSink.cs`) plus their production call sites and generated-client updates. None of it is Story 12.15 work — `git log` confirms no Story 12.15 commit touches any of it. Its production files fall outside every one of this pass's five review-chunk boundaries (none scope into `Gateway/Status/**` or `Lifecycle/Workflows/**`), so they were never going to be reviewed here. Its test files, however, live in the same `Hexalith.ChatBot.Server.Tests` assembly reviewed as chunk D and were swept into that diff by directory boundary alone: `tests/Hexalith.ChatBot.Server.Tests/Gateway/CommandGatewayTests.cs`, `tests/Hexalith.ChatBot.Server.Tests/Lifecycle/CorrectionPropagationCoordinatorTests.cs`, `tests/Hexalith.ChatBot.Server.Tests/Lifecycle/DaprWorkflowDuplicateInstanceDetectorTests.cs` (new file), `tests/Hexalith.ChatBot.Server.Tests/Lifecycle/OperationStatusWorkflowStatusSinkTests.cs` (new file). Kept as-is, not part of Story 12.15's File List or work, for the same reason as the DI line above; listed here so the commit's full contents stay disclosed.

## Change Log

- 2026-08-01 — v0.1: Ratified Story 12.15 live-recovery trust boundary, closed fault matrix, sandbox suitability, evidence retention, and production-equivalence residuals; added executable architecture guards (Task 1).
- 2026-08-01 — v0.2: Added the safe live-validation configuration, dedicated tenant identity, deterministic isolated dataset, report sink, and metadata-only evidence-manifest contracts (Task 2).
- 2026-08-01 — v0.3: Implemented real EventStore stop/start continuity and topology-composed M365 subscription recovery through the existing coordinator (Task 3).
- 2026-08-01 — v0.4: Implemented immutable-source-only projection rebuild with isolated partitioning, structural digests, and cleanup evidence (Task 4).
- 2026-08-01 — v0.5: Implemented all six closed scoped-outage drivers with independent-control and post-recovery assertions (Task 5).
- 2026-08-01 — v0.6: Added stable jobs, fail-closed evidence evaluation, and required CI/release workflow lanes (release: per-commit non-cancelling concurrency) with always-retained artifacts (Task 6).
- 2026-08-01 — v0.7: Hardened double opt-in, exact tenant/dataset checks, per-scenario timeout/restoration behavior, DAPR multi-sidecar topology, and authenticated actor-beta control proof (Tasks 3-7).
- 2026-08-01 — v0.8: Completed the passing nine-scenario local Tier-3 diagnostic run and captured measured A10/NFR57/NFR41 evidence (Task 7).
- 2026-08-01 — v0.9: Recorded the initial local A10 evidence, updated the decision/ADR/deferral records, recorded four durable production-equivalence residuals, and completed full regression verification (Task 8).
- 2026-08-01 — v1.0: Closed all implementation tasks, synchronized sprint status to `review`, and completed the Dev Agent Record for review handoff.
- 2026-08-01 — v1.1: Resolved the 17 returned review decisions/patches with persisted projection exercises, real dependency seams and monitoring, durable state checks, actual dataset materialization, token refresh, strict enum coverage, test-only topology composition, corrected workflow/governance records, and passing remediated Tier-3 evidence; A10 remains provisional pending a retained hosted artifact.
- 2026-08-01 — v1.2: Round-2 code review of the evidence-integrity layer (chunk 1 of the diff). Applied 14 of 22 patches — A10 provisional wording in `RecoveryTargets`, resolved commit/Aspire provenance, published `MeasurableRecoveryCeilingSeconds`, polled absence windows in place of fixed sleeps, confirmed EventStore stop before probing, server-side mailbox-token admission, guarded continuity cleanup `finally`, erase-before-verify with attachment-set key coverage, point-of-use identity control bearer, distinct listener-readiness budget, CSPRNG secrets, pooled sandbox HttpClient, verified fault injection, and a derived storage tenant governed by the single `ReplayTenantPolicy` predicate. Six patches were deliberately left for the decision-1/decision-2 rework. Status returned to `in-progress`: the four evidence-integrity root causes (unfalsifiable scope containment, stub-only dependencies, constant NFR59 flags, clock-read NFR41 latency) plus the RPO/rebuild tautologies remain open.
- 2026-08-01 — v1.3: Round-3 code review of the shipped product surface (chunk 2) and full remediation of all 16 items. The release gate now runs as a separate `live-recovery-evidence-gate` job in both workflows, reading only the uploaded artifact via `download-artifact` and evaluating out of process against a fresh wall clock (`LiveRecoveryEvidenceGateReplayTests`); `semantic-release` depends on that gate rather than on the run that produced the evidence. Gate thresholds moved out of the run's own envelope into `LiveRecoveryValidationGatePolicy` (`evaluatedAtUtc` and `ExpectedProjectionDatasets`; `LatestAttemptCompletedSuccessfully` and `AlertsDeliveredByJob` remain producer-supplied, now via `attempt.summary.json` — see the round-5 patch), and the gate now anchors allowed targets to `RecoveryTargets`, requires a per-job measurement vocabulary, requires the live `DriverMode`, requires cross-manifest tenant/dataset/commit coherence, and discloses a target wider than the lane's measurable ceiling through a new non-blocking `ClaimLimitationReasons` channel. Also: strict client enum rejection extended to enum collections via `ItemConverter` (16 previously-unprotected properties) with array deserialize coverage; `ReplayTenantPolicy` rejects the bare `replay-test:` prefix; `WorkflowTimeout` bounded by a `RunnerBudget` and cross-validated against the sweep's scenario count; the continuity sweep made deterministically ordered like its scoped-outage sibling; `SweepOrder` duplicates rejected; dataset descriptor compares the configured partition; evidence-retention failures carry a distinct deviation token; manifest ceiling finite-checked and gate null-safe; the realm client secret charset-validated with the rendered realm written user-only and deleted on exit; least-privilege `permissions` on both destructive jobs; release concurrency keyed by `github.ref`; and the AppHost `Dapr:InternalGrpcPorts` knob, access-control attachment scope, and grant-expiry format change disclosed (the access-control change is not a widening — those sidecars previously carried no Dapr config, whose default is already allow).
- 2026-08-01 — v1.4: Round-4 code review of the governance documents (chunk 3), completing the three-chunk pass. All four review decisions were delegated to the reviewer and applied with the 17 patch findings. Evidence: the cited local run's id and every measured figure (RPO/RTO, rebuild duration, scope-recording latencies) were **withdrawn** from `prd.md`, `.decision-log.md`, `addendum.md` and the predecessor ADRs — its manifests carry no `MeasurableRecoveryCeilingSeconds` and are rejected by the gate this change ships, so the bundle is unreplayable rather than merely unretained. Retirement: narrowed to what is proven — the live-driver deferral stays retired for `eventstore-outage`, the M365 subscription boundary, `identity` and `graph`, and for the projection store round-trip; re-opened for the four sandbox-exercised dependencies, the NFR59 safety flags, NFR58 observed scope (all six), NFR41 latency (all six), and the NFR57 equivalence verdict. Governance: the 180-second measurable recovery ceiling is now disclosed in the PRD A10 row, decision log, addendum and ADR, with a new `RV-MEASURABLE-CEILING` residual; the gate's ceiling claim-limitation was extended beyond the `rto` key so NFR57 is covered; the 8-day `MaximumEvidenceAge` is stated beside the 30-day retention; an explicit **un-ratification** transition and a fifth "remain provisional" A10 branch were added to the story and `epics.md` AC2. The ADR returned to `Proposed` with the architecture guard relaxed to accept either status. Also corrected: the controller-secret provenance claim, the tenant-alias "cannot resolve to either control tenant" claim, the cleanup stop-ship claim, the two PRD M2 requirement lines that had been rewritten into progress notes, the addendum's misattached single-source-of-truth blockquote, the `architecture.md`/`epics.md` A10 wording, the six-record dataset claim, the two split ledger sections, the missing `ContinuityDrillScenarios.cs` File List entry, and this log's own ordering. Two items deferred with owners; no sign-off recorded — A10 stays provisional and the story stays `in-progress`.
- 2026-08-02 — v1.5: Round-5 code review of the round-3/round-4 remediation itself — the only surface no adversarial layer had seen. All four delegated decisions and all 20 patches applied. **Gate:** the required lanes now pass `--settings live-recovery.runsettings` with `TreatNoTestsAsError`, because `dotnet test --filter` exits 0 on a zero-match filter, so renaming either lane's test turned a required job into a silent no-op that still reported success; the gate now requires the FULL per-job assertion vocabulary (`RequiredAssertionsFor`) and stop-ships on a false safety invariant (`SafetyAssertionsFor`), closing the hole where `fault-observed: false` beside verdict `met` passed with no reason code — `duration-within-target` and `scope-recorded-within-target` deliberately stay in the deviation channel per Task 6; `LiveRecoveryValidationGatePolicy` gained `ExpectedDatasetVersion`, `MinimumDatasetVolume`, `RequiredRepositoryCommit` and `MaximumMeasurableRecoveryCeilingSeconds`, so a one-record dataset, an inflated ceiling or evidence from an unrelated commit no longer passes on cross-manifest coherence alone; non-canonical measurement keys are rejected; and the round-4 ceiling disclosure was narrowed from every key to the two the restoration budget actually bounds (`rto`, `rebuild-duration`) — judging `rpo` and `scope-recording-latency` against a restoration budget was a category error emitted on every passing run. The dead `RecoveryTimeMeasurementKey` constant and its contradicting doc are gone. **Options:** `PerScenarioTimeout >= MaxRto` removed — it promised measurement through the 4-hour target while being unsatisfiable for nine serial scenarios inside a sub-`RunnerBudget` workflow — and replaced by a serial fair-share bound with overflow guarding; no `RestorationTimeout` floor was invented (round 2's reasoning stands). **Workflows:** release concurrency keyed by `github.sha` rather than `github.ref`, which did not prevent the pending-cancellation it was added for since every push to `main` shares a ref (accepted cost: concurrent rather than queued runs); the CI jobs renamed so branch protection can no longer match the skipped CI copy against a required release check; policy env vars wired in both lanes. **Also:** manifest artifact locators now name the artifact the workflow actually uploads (all three fixtures used a third, nonexistent prefix); the rendered Keycloak realm is created 0600 via `CreateTempSubdirectory` + `FileMode.CreateNew` + `UnixCreateMode` instead of 0644-then-chmod in a PID-keyed path; `ContinuityDrillScenarios.All` rejects duplicates like its sibling; `StorageTenantFor` rejects a suffix still carrying `:`; `TotalVolume` is checked; the strict-enum policy covers `IDictionary<,>`-valued enums and rejects an empty string for a nullable enum; the gate's max-age override fails closed on a malformed value; `Enabled` is derived and the attempt summary is written before the outcome assertions so the gate's disabled/incomplete branches are reachable from a real artifact; sink coverage must be stated rather than defaulted. **Tests:** gate fixtures state canonical targets as literals instead of calling `CanonicalTargetsFor` (which made `target_not_canonical` unfalsifiable), with one new test pinning that function to `RecoveryTargets`; new coverage for the assertion vocabulary, policy anchoring, ceiling scoping, non-finite ceilings, sweep-order distinctness and identity-last at runtime, tenant derivation, volume overflow and the enum policy; architecture guards now assert the runsettings, the sha-keyed group, the distinct CI job names, and that `RunnerBudget`/`Cadence` match the workflow values they describe. **Governance:** Task 2 and the evidence-retention obligation narrowed to the two artifact kinds the lane produces, with new residual `RV-EVIDENCE-KINDS`; `RV-MEASURABLE-CEILING` corrected for the removed rule; v1.3's threshold-relocation claim corrected to the two of four inputs actually moved; the 42 story-13.9 screenshots in commit `1838eba` disclosed. Verified: solution build 0 warnings/0 errors; Server 1740/1740, Architecture 73/73, Conformance 98/98, AppHost 14/14, Client 44/44, Integration 99 passed / 5 skipped / 0 failed across three consecutive runs. The live Tier-3 test remains skipped in this environment and was **not** executed, so none of this has live-topology proof. The 14 open round-2 items are untouched and remain the gate on `done`.
- 2026-08-02 — v1.6: Round-2 evidence-integrity remediation (Jerome's round-2 decisions 1-4, plus 6 patches; 12 of 14 fully resolved, 2 documented as residual). **Scoped-outage falsifiability (decision 1):** `RecoveryDependencyExercise` now routes `ai-provider` through a real `AcceptedCommandDispatcher` (via a real `ChatBotCommandAdmissionPipeline` admission first, so `ApprovalResult`/`RiskClassification` are genuinely populated), `audit-store` through the real `ChatBotCommandAdmissionPipeline`'s fail-closed pre-commit gate, and `attachment-processing` through the real `AttachmentCaptureCoordinator` against a seeded projection candidate and a new minimal-but-real `RecoveryFolderStore` (the production `UnavailableFolderStore` always fails, which would mask the dependency under test). All non-target admission stages are the real production types constructed directly (`RecoverySandbox` already has `InternalsVisibleTo` from `Hexalith.ChatBot.Server`); only the one dependency under test per scenario is a controllable double. NFR59 safety flags now compare the ground-truth fault switch against the real orchestrator's own decision (admission accept/reject, the dispatcher's actually-submitted `ExecutionRecord.Outcome`, the coordinator's actual `StoredCount`) instead of a leaf type mirroring its own fault check. `command-execution` was left unchanged (already real). **Scope/latency (NFR58/NFR41):** `RecoveryScopeObservationMonitor.ScopeFor` — byte-identical to `LiveScopedOutageInjectionDriver.ExpectedScope` — replaced with `ScopeForSignal`, keyed by a `FaultSignalCode` the failing component itself returns, independent of the `dependency` token both used before; the monitor moved from an instant same-process `Channel` handoff to a `PeriodicTimer` poll (200ms) so the recorded scope latency is a genuine non-zero, boundable value instead of a same-tick echo. **RPO (decision 2, continuity half):** `SeedCommittedOperationAsync` seeds 3 independently timestamped governed notes instead of a hardcoded `RecoveryOperationCheckpoint(1, ...)`; end-state now counts how many of the 3 genuinely reconstruct, making a real partial-loss outcome reachable instead of a trivial 0-or-1 boolean. **Projection rebuild (decision 2, rebuild half):** the rebuild path no longer writes an identity copy (`source with { TenantId = partitionTenant }`); it reconstructs each immutable source into a `MailboxMessageIntakeCaptured` event and replays it through the real `AssociationProjectionHandler.HandleAsync` — the same production code that turns a captured mailbox event into a projection — so a handler regression is a reachable divergence. **RTO (decision 3) and storage-tenant guard (decision 4):** verified already resolved by round-3/4/5 remediation on files this pass otherwise left alone — `RecoveryTargets.MaxRto`'s doc comment already states the lane "cannot demonstrate a miss" of the 4-hour target, and `RecoverySandboxTopologyComposer.AddRecoverySandbox` already hard-throws unless the physical tenant equals `ReplayTenantPolicy.StorageTenantFor(logicalTenant)`; the round-2 checkboxes were simply never re-checked. **Patches:** threaded the real cleanup outcome into `CleanupComplete` for all three drivers (`IRecoverySandboxOperations.Cleanup*Async` now return `ValueTask<bool>`);
